# Most Recommended Books — Full Index
> Human-curated book database of 760+ people recs, 1,300+ lists, 39,000+ series, and 430+ summaries. Every recommendation is verified against its original source (interview, podcast, post) and quoted on the page.

Data last updated 2026-07-21. Each link below is a live content page. For programmatic access (free JSON API + MCP server) see [Developers](https://mostrecommendedbooks.com/developers); methodology at [Methodology](https://mostrecommendedbooks.com/methodology).
